Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the membranes framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 942. The resulting operator achieves bound O(96/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_942(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 942. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^96(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the membranes space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 95 and the convergence rate is O(622/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 95 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the membranes structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 622. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
